| `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:285` | `mandatory` | `manual_review` | Figure 1 shows this progression. | `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:295` | `mandatory` | `runtime_governance_review` | Software Industrialisation shall be established as a federated enterprise capability: centrally coordinated, jointly developed and locally executed. Enterprise governance sets direction and decides the capability portfolio. A lightweight Software Industrialisation Office integrates the system. Named Enterprise Capability Leads remain accountable for shared capability outcomes. Business Units, programmes and engineering teams retain accountability for their products, resources, local implementation and delivery. |
| `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:297` | `mandatory` | `runtime_governance_review` | This allocation is only credible if responsibility is matched by authority. The Steering Board must operate under an explicit executive mandate. Capability Leads require documented decision rights within an approved scope. Business Unit representatives need time, access and authority to secure decisions in their organisations. Business Unit Leads and Engineering Heads must accept the capacity and adoption obligations assigned to them. Without these conditions, enterprise governance becomes voluntary coordination and can be ignored whenever local pressure increases. |
| `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:299` | `mandatory` | `manual_review` | Governance alone is insufficient. | `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:307` | `mandatory` | `manual_review` | The required answer is neither unmanaged local independence nor a central delivery hierarchy. It is a governed federation that provides common direction and enterprise capability while preserving the accountability of the organisations that deliver products and programmes. The model connects the existing distribution of responsibility to explicit enterprise decision rights instead of attempting to conceal or remove it. |
| `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:311` | `mandatory` | `evidence_contracts, runtime_governance_review` | Figure 2 summarises the operating model. It shall be read as an authority and accountability model, not as a new disciplinary hierarchy. | `SW-INDUSTRIALISATION-OM-CAND-001` | `candidate` | `docs/governance/source-documents/Software_Industrialisation_Enterprise_Operating_Model_revised_extended_scope_polished.md:330` | `mandatory` | `runtime_governance_review` | The five initial domains are a capability portfolio, not five new organisational silos and not a quota to be distributed one per Business Unit. ## Review Notes

- This is a keyword-based normative statement delta, not a final legal or architecture decision.
- The requirement intake inventory scans registered source text and separates source-identified IDs from inferred prose candidates; repeated or overlapping sources are counted per source.
- Prose extraction is a review aid: inferred_candidate items may be context or explanation and require source-owner confirmation.
- Candidate-intake records whose underlying source text is withheld are listed as unscanned and are not counted.
- Changed, added, and removed mandatory or prohibition statements require human architecture-owner review.
- Removed statements may indicate content deleted from the candidate or moved into companion documents; confirm against the full architecture source set before deciding.
- Author-identified requirements with a source ID receive intake priority p1; prose-only inferred_candidate items receive p2 and require owner confirmation.
- Intake identification class and priority are separate from delta review priority and source approval status.
- No source document status, lineage, runtime governance, policy, schema, or release package is changed by this report.
